官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Quantum Generative Diffusion Model: A Fully Quantum-Mechanical Model for Generating Quantum State Ensemble

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3718311 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Mixed quantum states are the native description of many physically important quantum systems, making their generation a fundamental task in quantum information processing. However, constructing a diffusion process that generates density operators while keeping every reverse step physically valid remains nontrivial. This work introduces Quantum Generative Diffusion Model (QGDM), a fully quantum-mechanical model whose forward and backward processes are grounded in quantum channel theory. Through a non-unitary forward process, any target quantum state can be transformed into a completely mixed state. A trainable backward process recovers the former from the latter. We introduce partial trace to make the backward process non-unitary, and share parameters across timesteps by incorporating temporal information as an input. We present QGDM’s resource-efficient version to reduce auxiliary qubits while preserving generative capabilities. We theoretically analyze the denoising design, showing it avoids a low-loss shortcut that traps training and cause generation failure. Simulations confirm that QGDM outperforms quantum generative adversarial networks on random pure- and mixed-state generation, with better noise robustness than other quantum generative models and a task-specialized approach for practical Gibbs state generation. Hence, QGDM provides a channel-based diffusion framework for learning fixed mixed-state targets, extending quantum generative modeling toward realistic quantum information settings.

M3R++: Masked Token Mixup and Dual Cross-Modal Masking for Zero-Shot Learning

Qiangchang Wang; Yuchi Xiao; Rushi Lan; Yilong Yin; Huimin Lu

卷期
V48 · I11
门控

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

M3R++: Masked Token Mixup and Dual Cross-Modal Masking for Zero-Shot Learning

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3724025 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Zero-shot learning (ZSL) aims to recognize novel class objects by transferring semantic knowledge from seen to unseen classes. However, existing methods often misclassify unseen class images as seen classes due to a lack of robust correspondences between visual features and semantic attributes. To address these issues, we propose M3R++, a novel framework that utilizes masked token mixup and dual cross-modal masking to enhance zero-shot learning by building strong connections between semantic attributes and visual clues, thereby reducing the bias towards seen classes. The M3R++ framework consists of four key components: Random Token Mixup (RTM), Unseen Class Detection (UCD), Dual Cross-modality Masked auto-encoders (DCM), and Diverse Prompt Learning (DPL). First, RTM can broaden the representation space of unseen classes by mixing visual tokens from different class samples and combining their semantic attributes to generate synthetic unseen class samples. Second, UCD adaptively assigns different entropy values to distinguish between seen and unseen class samples, reducing classification bias. Third, DCM can establish robust relationships between semantic attributes and visual knowledge through vision-guided and attribute-guided cross-modal masking. Fourth, DPL introduces a divergence loss to encourage diversity among a set of visual prompts, thereby providing rich visual clues for accurately mining correspondences between semantic attributes and visual knowledge. Extensive experiments on four challenging datasets demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of the proposed M3R++ model.

Stitching Misaligned Multi-Spectral Images by Versatile Transformation: A Streamlined Solution and Applications

Zhiying Jiang; Zengxi Zhang; Jinyuan Liu; Xin Fan; Risheng Liu

门控

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Stitching Misaligned Multi-Spectral Images by Versatile Transformation: A Streamlined Solution and Applications

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3719795 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Multi-spectral image stitching reconstructs the informative and broader scene by aligning the image pairs captured from different viewpoints. This task is significantly limited by the registered sources. Stitching accuracy deteriorates notably with misaligned multi-modality images. To address the challenges imposed by the registration requirement, we propose robust multi-spectral image stitching for misaligned infrared-visible pairs, in which the intrinsic attributes of multi-modality registration and multi-view alignment are employed concurrently, rather than independent processing. Specifically, the proposed method develops a hierarchical versatile transformation to conduct the correspondence matching progressively, where the sparse foundational homography is utilized for global adjustment and point-flexible splines are applied for local fine-tuning. In the registration of infrared and visible images, a dual consistency-driven modality transfer is introduced to alleviate feature variance and facilitate registration within the shared information matching. During cross-view alignment, multi-spectral image pairs are encoded into a latent domain to exploit the complementary information adaptively, and the planar deformation is conducted through correspondence matching processes identical to those in the registration procedure. In this way, by leveraging the consistency between multi-modality and multi-view matching, we achieve a robust reconstruction of the multi-spectral panorama. Extensive experiments in registration and stitching demonstrate the superiority of our method.

MambaX: Image Super-Resolution With State Predictive Control

Chenyu Li; Danfeng Hong; Bing Zhang; Zhaojie Pan; Naoto Yokoya; Jocelyn Chanussot

V48 · I11

The Hitchhiker’s Guide to Autonomous Research: A Survey of Scientific Agents

Xinming Wang; Jian Xu; Sheng Lian; Yi Chen; Haiyang Guo; Fei Zhu; Yuanqi Shao; Minsi Ren; Hongzhu Yi; Aslan H. Feng; Hongming Yang; Tailin Wu; Han Hu; Shiming Xiang; Xu-Yao Zhang; Cheng-Lin Liu

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3721500
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

The Hitchhiker’s Guide to Autonomous Research: A Survey of Scientific Agents

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3721500 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

The advancement of LLM-based agents is redefining AI for Science (AI4S) by enabling autonomous scientific research. Prominent LLMs exhibited expertise across multiple domains, catalysing constructions of domain-specialised scientific agents. Nevertheless, the profound epistemic and methodological gaps between AI and the natural sciences still impede the systematic design, training, and validation of these agents. This survey bridges the existing gap by presenting a comprehensive blueprint for scientific agents’ design. It first clarifies the concept of scientific agents and distinguishes them from general-purpose agents in terms of their goal orientation, workflow embedding, and scientific commitments. It then introduces a unified taxonomy based on capability envelope and capability maturity, characterizing both the scope of scientific workflow coverage and the reliability of agent behavior under realistic research conditions. Building on this taxonomy, the survey further connects scientific agent design with the research life cycle by reviewing construction strategies, capability enhancement methods, evaluation paradigms, and future challenges. This unified perspective aims to provide practical guidance for designing domain-specific scientific agents and to promote the convergence of AI research and natural scientific discovery. To support long-term progress, we curate a live repository (https://github.com/gudehhh666/Awesome_Scientific_Agent Awesome Scientific Agent) that continuously aggregates emerging methods, benchmarks, and best practices.

IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

ROBIN++: Unified Copyright Protection and Tamper Localization for Diffusion Models via Dual-Domain Synergistic Watermarking

Huayang Huang; Siqi Zeng; Qian Wang; Bo Du; Yu Wu

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3723036
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

ROBIN++: Unified Copyright Protection and Tamper Localization for Diffusion Models via Dual-Domain Synergistic Watermarking

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3723036 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Digital watermarking provides a promising solution for verifying the provenance and integrity of generated content. However, existing methods often suffer from an inherent trade-off between copyright robustness and localization sensitivity due to the entanglement of conflicting signals within a single domain. In this paper, we propose ROBIN++, a paradigm that shifts from single-domain entanglement to a Dual-Domain Synergistic design. Our key insight is to explicitly decouple robustness and fragility during injection by assigning them to distinct domains, while re-coupling them for mutual reinforcement during verification. Specifically, we introduce an Adversarial Optimization Algorithm to inject a frequency-domain copyright watermark during generation, maximizing extraction robustness without visual artifacts. Complementarily, a Content-Aware Perturbation generator is integrated into the latent space to inject the spatial-domain localization watermark, ensuring sensitivity to spatial tampering with minimal interference to the copyright watermark. During verification, we propose a dual-branch synergistic detector that facilitates Frequency-to-Spatial Synergy, where robust spectral priors act as global anchors for local anomaly detection. Conversely, a Spatial-to-Frequency Refinement strategy is employed to rectify corrupted latent features using predicted tamper masks. Extensive experiments show that ROBIN++ significantly outperforms existing approaches in both copyright recovery and tamper localization accuracy, effectively mitigating the long-standing trade-off between the two tasks.

IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

Kolmogorov-Arnold Causal Generative Models

Alejandro Almodovar; Mar Elizo; Patricia A. Apellaniz; Santiago Zazo; Juan Parras

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3721159
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Kolmogorov-Arnold Causal Generative Models

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3721159 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Causal generative models provide a principled framework for answering observational, interventional, and counterfactual queries from observational data. However, many deep causal models rely on expressive architectures with opaque mechanisms, limiting auditability in high-stakes domains. This paper introduces the Kolmogorov-Arnold causal generative model, a mixed-type tabular causal generative model that parameterizes each structural equation with a Kolmogorov–Arnold Network. The resulting mechanisms can be inspected through symbolic approximations and parent-child visualizations while preserving query-agnostic causal generative semantics. The model includes a validation pipeline based on distributional matching and independence diagnostics of inferred exogenous variables, allowing adequacy to be assessed using observational data alone. Experimentally, on eleven synthetic mixed-type benchmarks, the Kolmogorov-Arnold causal generative model is not statistically distinguishable from the best state-of-the-art model: aggregate post-hoc p -value of 0.69. On the semi-synthetic Sachs’ additive dataset, with 11 variables, the model obtains a p -value of 0.18 against the best model. A real- world cardiovascular case study demonstrates the interpretability workflow, showing how learned mechanisms can be simplified into structural equations and visualized to audit parent–child effects across the causal system. This suggest that expressive causal generative modeling and functional transparency can be achieved jointly, supporting trustworthy deployment in tabular decision-making settings.

IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

Handling Learnwares From Heterogeneous Feature and Label Spaces With Explicit Label Exploitation

Peng Tan; Zhi-Hao Tan; Hai-Tian Liu; Zhi-Hua Zhou

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3723272
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Handling Learnwares From Heterogeneous Feature and Label Spaces With Explicit Label Exploitation

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3723272 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

‘‘Learnware = Model + Specification”. The learnware paradigm aims to help users reuse existing high-performing models instead of training models from scratch, where the specification characterizes a trained model’s capability and utility without exposing raw data. Numerous learnwares are hosted by a learnware dock system, which identifies helpful learnwares according to user task requirements and returns suitable models for reuse. In practice, learnwares that exactly match a user’s feature space are often rare, while models from heterogeneous feature spaces, or even heterogeneous label spaces, may still be reusable. The central challenge is to characterize and compare the reusability of such heterogeneous models within a unified representation space. This paper finds that label information, especially model outputs, is crucial but previously insufficiently used. We exploit model outputs to evolve specifications into a unified space, thereby constructing a unified capability representation of heterogeneous models that characterizes their predictive behavior across diverse feature and label spaces. To instantiate this idea, we extend the specification implementation to encode model-output behavior more effectively and prevent it from being overwhelmed by high-dimensional features. Based on the unified capability representation, we identify learnwares by matching the conditional distributions induced by model outputs with the true distributions of user tasks, enabling learnwares to be reused beyond their original purposes. Experiments show that the proposed method can effectively identify and assemble learnwares from diverse feature and label spaces, even when no submitted learnware is explicitly tailored to the user task.

Rethinking Multi-Modal Image Super-Resolution: The Key Role of Cross-Modal Consistency Prior

卷期

NEW_ARTICLE

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Rethinking Multi-Modal Image Super-Resolution: The Key Role of Cross-Modal Consistency Prior

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3723450 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

For multi-modal image super-resolution (MISR), exploring cross-modal consistency is of vital importance. However, most existing consistency priors struggle to preserve high-frequency components and fail to provide generalizable regularization, often resulting in blurred edges or inaccurate textures. In this paper, we revisit the pivotal role of consistency prior in MISR task and present an important finding: the modality gap in the Laplacian response between guidance and target images does not conform to the widely adopted Gaussian or Laplacian distributions. Instead, it aligns better with T-distribution. Based on this insight, we propose a T-distribution formed Laplacian response Consistency (TLC) model. This model integrates a T-distribution based Multi-modal Consistency (TMC) prior with a learnable regularization term that operates between the guidance and target images. Additionally, we introduce a Multiplicative Degradation (MD) matrix to model the degradation process from high-resolution (HR) to low-resolution (LR) target images, thereby enabling adaptive non-uniform degradation modeling. The iterative optimization steps of the TLC model are subsequently unfolded into an interpretable network, termed TLCNet. The performance of TLCNet is evaluated on nine datasets across three MISR tasks, demonstrating its superior super-resolution performance compared to other state-of-the-art approaches. The visualization of intermediate features and the causal analysis of the guidance image further confirm its good interpretability.

IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

Robust Audio-Visual Question Answering With Missing Modality in Training and Testing

Jinxing Zhou; Zhangbin Li; Di Hu; Yanghao Zhou; Rao Muhammad Anwer; Hisham Cholakkal; Dan Guo

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3723653
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Robust Audio-Visual Question Answering With Missing Modality in Training and Testing

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3723653 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Audio-Visual Question Answering (AVQA) methods typically assume both audio and visual modalities are available. In practice, however, an audio or visual stream may be unavailable because of passive signal loss, such as hardware or transmission failures, or intentional removal, such as withholding visual information for privacy. We formulate Training-time Modality-Missing AVQA (TM-AVQA), a setting where modality-complete, audio-missing, and visual-missing samples occur during both training and testing. To address this, we develop an AVQA-specific two-stage framework adapting cross-modal reconstruction to TM-AVQA constraints. In Stage-I, a reconstruction network infers missing-modality features from the available modality and question text. A Dense Temporal-scale Reconstruction (DTR) module aggregates multi-granularity information, while a Multimodal Dependency Modeling (MDM) module captures audio-visual-question dependencies. To supervise reconstruction without target features, we introduce Cross-Modal Relation-based Contrastive Learning (CMR-CL) to exploit multimodal relations, and Cross-Sample Relation-based Pseudo-label Learning (CSR-PL) to construct pseudo-targets from modality-complete samples. In Stage-II, the frozen reconstruction network combines with existing AVQA backbones for answer prediction. We construct controlled TM-AVQA variants of the MUSIC-AVQA, MUSIC-AVQA-R, and AVQA datasets. Experiments across multiple backbones, missing-modality conditions, and missing rates show robust improvements over existing baselines. Additional ablations validate the effectiveness of our task-specific designs, reconstruction quality, efficiency, and transferability to audio-visual recognition tasks.

Hyperbolic Hierarchical 3D Scene Representations for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation

Haoran Hou; Mingtao Feng; Qing Zhu; Zijie Wu; Jianqiao Luo; Min Liu; Yaonan Wang

卷期
V48 · I11
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1109/tpami.2026.3721639
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面www.computer.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Hyperbolic Hierarchical 3D Scene Representations for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation

期刊：IEEE Transactions on Pattern Analysis and Machine Intelligence · DOI：10.1109/tpami.2026.3721639 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation aims to predict 3D objects and their predicates beyond the annotated label space. Compared to closed-set 3D scene graph generation methods, the open-vocabulary approach is more general, practical, and less dependent on labor-intensive ground truth annotations. Existing open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation methods rely on learning individual object and predicate features in the representation space while ignoring higher-level 3D scene representations, leading to overfitting and suboptimal performance. In this work, we propose a hyperbolic learning-based approach to address this problem by leveraging hyperbolic geometry to learn hierarchical 3D scene representations in the form of scene–region–instance, where the scene represents the complete 3D environment, a region contains related instances, and an instance corresponds to an individual object or predicate. Specifically, our method decomposes a 3D scene into a discrete hierarchy consisting of scene, region, and instance nodes, and embeds this hierarchy into a learned hyperbolic representation space. The learned hyperbolic embeddings are optimized in a bottom-up manner, where higher-level nodes are derived from their corresponding child nodes. The learned hierarchical 3D scene representations are incorporated as structural and semantic guidance for open-vocabulary 3D scene graph generation. We further observe that outliers in the form of erroneous hyperbolic embeddings can negatively impact hierarchical reasoning. To mitigate their negative impact, we present an enhancement strategy that learns an adaptive distance metric robust to the outliers over the learned hyperbolic representation space and subsequently improves overall performance. Extensive experiments on 3DSSG and ScanNet datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in 3D scene graph generation under closed-set, open-vocabulary, and zero-shot settings.

Healthcare service evaluation using disc spherical fuzzy confidence-level and Yager aggregation-based AHP-CRITIC-COPRAS approach

Prayosi Chatterjee; Mijanur Rahaman Seikh

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01267-3
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Healthcare service evaluation using disc spherical fuzzy confidence-level and Yager aggregation-based AHP-CRITIC-COPRAS approach

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01267-3 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Robust evaluation of healthcare services is essential for safeguarding the quality of care, expanding access, and allocating resources judiciously. It also aids in identifying strengths and weaknesses within healthcare services, enabling informed decisions to enhance overall patient care. To meet this need, a confidence level-integrated group decision-making framework is developed. The expert evaluations are modelled using disc spherical fuzzy numbers (DSFNs), an advanced extension of fuzzy theory known for its ability to effectively capture multidimensional uncertainty. This study introduces two novel Yager norm-based aggregation operators for DSFNs, which are embedded in the decision-making model. The weights of experts are determined using the Rank Order Centroid (ROC) method, while the subjective and objective criteria weights are determined using the Analytical Hierarchy Process (AHP) and Criteria Importance Through Intercriteria Correlation (CRITIC) method, respectively. The confidence levels of experts are included in the decision-making to ensure practical relevance. Lastly, the healthcare services are ranked using the Complex Proportional Assessment (COPRAS) method. The results indicate that communication, expertise, and equipment availability are of the highest importance. Among the hospitals evaluated, Hospital B is found to perform the best. The comparative analysis establishes the practicality of the model, while the sensitivity analysis assesses its robustness. The resulting model provides a transparent, versatile decision-support tool that can sharpen managerial insight and strengthen evidence-based policy in healthcare systems.

Automatic classification of rodent navigation strategies in the Barnes maze using geometric feature statistics

Vanessa Ramírez-Pérez; José A. Guerrero-Díaz-de-León; Jorge E. Macías-Díaz

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01277-1
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Automatic classification of rodent navigation strategies in the Barnes maze using geometric feature statistics

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01277-1 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

This paper presents a machine learning framework for the automatic classification of rodent navigation strategies in the Barnes maze using geometric feature statistics extracted from trajectory data. Five geometric descriptors are computed from each trajectory. Five statistical measures are then calculated for each descriptor, resulting in a 25-feature representation. A classification methodology based on the Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA) algorithm is proposed, with experimental results demonstrating adequate performance across a range of classification metrics with a mean accuracy of 83.68%, providing an objective and efficient alternative to manual behavioral assessment.

Stochastic Nesterov accelerated gradient with adaptive Polyak step size: convergence analysis in non-convex problems and applications in deep learning

Shuyin Deng; Yong Li; Zhongyu Mo; Heshu Yang; Canjie Huang; Gonglin Yuan

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01279-z
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Stochastic Nesterov accelerated gradient with adaptive Polyak step size: convergence analysis in non-convex problems and applications in deep learning

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01279-z · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

The stochastic Nesterov accelerated gradient (SNAG) method is widely used in large-scale machine learning. It computes the stochastic gradient at a look-ahead point generated by the momentum history. The performance of SNAG is sensitive to the choice of step size, and a suitable step size can improve its numerical convergence behavior. Therefore, designing an effective step-size strategy is important for stochastic accelerated optimization. This paper focuses on the SNAG framework and proposes an adaptive step-size scheme. Specifically, we introduce a stochastic Nesterov accelerated gradient method with an adaptive Polyak step size, termed SNAG-APS. SNAG-APS evaluates both the stochastic loss and gradient at the Nesterov extrapolation point and uses a momentum-dependent scaling factor in the adaptive step size. The main contributions of this paper include: (i) We design SNAG-APS by combining a Polyak-type adaptive step size with stochastic Nesterov acceleration. (ii) Under standard smoothness assumptions and the weak growth condition, we establish a nonconvex stationarity bound for SNAG-APS, which contains a decreasing term and a residual term. (iii) Experiments on classical machine learning datasets and deep neural network benchmarks show that SNAG-APS achieves competitive numerical performance compared with several optimization algorithms.

Evolutionary Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

Modified stochastic paint optimizer to define optimum parameters of lithium-ion battery model for electric vehicles

Ahmed M. Nassef; Hegazy Rezk; Kaiguang Wang; Fatma A. Hashim

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01266-4
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Modified stochastic paint optimizer to define optimum parameters of lithium-ion battery model for electric vehicles

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01266-4 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

In recent years, metaheuristic optimization algorithms have emerged as effective tools for identifying optimal parameter values. This paper presents an effective parameter identification method based on a newly proposed optimizer, namely the Modified Stochastic Paint Optimizer (mSPO). This algorithm is applied to identify the parameters of the Shepherd’s electric vehicle model. In this work, the original Stochastic Paint Optimizer (SPO) is enhanced by incorporating a memory-based elite solution guidance strategy to produce the mSPO. This modification helps to avoid the common pitfalls in high-dimensional optimization landscapes, such as premature convergence, and prevents the algorithm from becoming trapped in local minima. Additionally, the adaptive mutation strategy is employed to dynamically adjust the balance between exploration and exploitation based on the results of previous iterations. These improvements enable the mSPO to provide a faster and more accurate convergence curve, making it a highly effective tool for solving complex optimization problems such as battery parameter identification. The performance of the mSPO is compared with six other competitive algorithms, including the SPO, COOT, ROA, AOA, OOA, and PSO. The comparison encompasses optimizing ten different CEC2022 benchmark functions as well as the Li-ion battery models. Based on the average cost function, the mSPO is ranked first among the other optimizers. Furthermore, the identification method using the modified mSPO proved highly effective in ascertaining the battery’s unidentified characteristics. The lowest average cost function value of 0.01028 was attained by mSPO, followed by ROA at 0.01223, whereas the worst value of 0.07409 was obtained by AOA. Therefore, the outstanding results of the mSPO proved that it is a promising competitor.

Optimizing last-mile delivery: a fitness landscape analysis and hybrid ALNS-GA framework

Sepide Aghaie; Ehsan Memarpour; Behrooz Karimi; Hossein Akbaripour

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01274-4
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Optimizing last-mile delivery: a fitness landscape analysis and hybrid ALNS-GA framework

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01274-4 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

The rapid growth of e-commerce and retail distribution has increased the need for efficient last-mile delivery systems that jointly account for courier availability, order prioritization, and routing decisions. This study addresses this challenge by integrating courier assignment, order dispatching, and route planning within a unified optimization framework. A mixed-integer linear programming model is developed to represent the problem and provide optimal reference solutions for small-scale instances. Fitness landscape analysis is then used to characterize the underlying search space and identify the need for a search strategy that combines diversification and intensification. Based on these insights, a hybrid adaptive large neighborhood search–genetic algorithm is developed, while the standalone adaptive large neighborhood search and genetic algorithm are implemented as comparative approaches. Computational results show that all three methods provide high-quality solutions for small instances, while the hybrid approach consistently achieves better solution quality and robustness with competitive computational efficiency as problem complexity increases. Non-parametric statistical tests further support the observed performance differences. These findings are consistent with the search-design implications derived from the fitness landscape analysis, highlighting the benefit of combining population-based diversification with adaptive neighborhood-based intensification for complex last-mile delivery problems. Beyond algorithmic performance, the study compares open- and closed-loop routing strategies and shows that their relative advantages depend on operational conditions and order distribution. The results provide practical guidance for designing efficient last-mile delivery systems while demonstrating how landscape-informed hybrid optimization can support solution-method design for complex routing problems.

Evolutionary Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

A TLBO-GEP algorithm with weight domain to eliminate inferior genes and accelerate convergence for symbolic regression problems

Yang Yang; Yuan Wang; Kang Wang

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01262-8
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

A TLBO-GEP algorithm with weight domain to eliminate inferior genes and accelerate convergence for symbolic regression problems

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01262-8 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

Symbolic Regression (SR) problems are extensively solved using Gene Expression Programming (GEP); however, traditional GEP often suffers from premature convergence and a high proportion of invalid gene expressions. To address this issue, this study proposes a novel algorithm aiming to significantly improve convergence speed and model learning efficiency. The proposed methodology introduces an altered chromosome structure by adding a gene weight domain. This domain is dynamically optimized using the Teaching–Learning-Based Optimization (TLBO) algorithm, which evaluates and refines weights via a two-stage teacher-student mechanism. The algorithm was evaluated against 16 varied standard SR cases and a practical H62 brass corner milling application. Experimental results demonstrate that TLBO-GEP eliminates inferior genes and drastically accelerates convergence. Specifically, in the engineering case, TLBO-GEP achieved an exceptionally low average prediction relative error of 2.59%, significantly outperforming standard GEP (9.27%), BPNN (11.80%), and SVM (6.01%). In conclusion, while facing minor limitations with deeply nested exponential functions, the integration of TLBO into GEP provides a highly robust, efficient, and precise mathematical modeling tool for complex predictive applications.

Evolutionary Intelligence · ONLINE_FIRST_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

A novel hybrid and quantum-augmented variant of particle swarm optimization algorithm to solve large-scale and non-convex economic emission dispatch problems in power systems

Lida Mirzaei; Bader A. Alshammari; Pratham Adhikari; Hieu Nguyen; Ehsan Naderi

卷期
V19 · I6
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1007/s12065-026-01272-6
证据层级
OFFICIAL_TOC_OR_CROSSREF_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-10-24 12:34 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

A novel hybrid and quantum-augmented variant of particle swarm optimization algorithm to solve large-scale and non-convex economic emission dispatch problems in power systems

期刊：Evolutionary Intelligence · DOI：10.1007/s12065-026-01272-6 · 语言：en · 摘要来源：出版方摘要

This paper presents a novel hybrid optimization algorithm to solve large-scale and non-convex Economic Emission Dispatch (EED) problems in power systems. The proposed algorithm incorporates quantum-inspired operators into the velocity update mechanism of standard Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithm, aiming to enhance population diversity and convergence efficiency in high-dimensional search spaces. In addition, the proposed algorithm is enhanced via a fuzzy interface system to dynamically adopt the inertia weight of the particles in the course of iterations. Finally, it is hybridized with Grey Wolf Optimizer (GWO) to significantly improve the exploration and exploitation processes in the search space of the problem. Hence, this paper introduces a Hybrid Quantum-Augmented PSO-GWO (HQAPSO-GWO) algorithm to tackle one of the most important problems in power system operation. To model the realistic and complex characteristics of power generation, the cost function integrates Valve-Point Loading Effects (VPLEs), introducing non-convexity and multiple local minima into the problem landscape. The effectiveness of the proposed HQAPSO-GWO is evaluated on several unimodal and multi-modal benchmark optimization problems as well as different EED scenarios over a challenging 40-unit test system, demonstrating superior solution quality and computational performance compared to existing state-of-the-art algorithms. Simulation results confirm that the HQAPSO-GWO algorithm provides a robust and scalable solution methodology for modern EED problems, making it a promising approach for large-scale power system optimization. For instance, the proposed algorithm achieved a reduction of 2.4% in the total generation cost for the 40-unit system compared to existing algorithms, translating to an annual savings of over $25 M. Furthermore, it identified the optimal solution in almost 100% less computational time, highlighting its suitability for real-time applications by power system operators in daily operational planning.

官方论文页面link.springer.com · 论文页面
